Empirically, agents trained with Reward-Zero converge faster and achieve higher final success rates than conventional methods such as PPO with common reward-shaping baselines, successfully solving tasks that hand-designed rewards could not in some complex tasks. In addition, we develop a mini benchmark for the evaluation of completion sense during task execution via language embeddings. These results highlight the promise of language-driven implicit reward functions as a practical path toward more sample-efficient, generalizable, and scalable RL for embodied agents. Code will be released after peer review.

</details>


### [81] [TA-GGAD: Testing-time Adaptive Graph Model for Generalist Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09349)
*Xiong Zhang,Hong Peng,Changlong Fu,Xin Jin,Yun Yang,Cheng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文识别并建模了图异常检测中的“异常异质性”问题，提出了一种能够跨领域泛化的图基础模型，显著提升了通用图异常检测的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中跨领域的图异常行为（如虚假新闻、恶意交易）存在严重的特征差异（Domain Shift），导致现有检测模型在不同领域间的泛化能力受限。

Method: 定义并量化了“异常异质性”（Anomaly Disassortativity, AD）问题，并据此设计了一种全新的图基础模型，通过单阶段训练实现广义跨领域泛化。

Result: 在 14 个真实世界数据集上的实验证明，该模型在跨领域适配方面取得了突破，检测准确率达到 SOTA 水平。

Conclusion: 本研究提出的 AD 理论为通用图异常检测提供了新的理论视角，并为跨领域图学习奠定了实践基础。

Abstract: A significant number of anomalous nodes in the real world, such as fake news, noncompliant users, malicious transactions, and malicious posts, severely compromises the health of the graph data ecosystem and urgently requires effective identification and processing. With anomalies that span multiple data domains yet exhibit vast differences in features, cross-domain detection models face severe domain shift issues, which limit their generalizability across all domains. This study identifies and quantitatively analyzes a specific feature mismatch pattern exhibited by domain shift in graph anomaly detection, which we define as the \emph{Anomaly Disassortativity} issue ($\mathcal{AD}$). Based on the modeling of the issue $\mathcal{AD}$, we introduce a novel graph foundation model for anomaly detection. It achieves cross-domain generalization in different graphs, requiring only a single training phase to perform effectively across diverse domains. The experimental findings, based on fourteen diverse real-world graphs, confirm a breakthrough in the model's cross-domain adaptation, achieving a pioneering state-of-the-art (SOTA) level in terms of detection accuracy. In summary, the proposed theory of $\mathcal{AD}$ provides a novel theoretical perspective and a practical route for future research in generalist graph anomaly detection (GGAD). The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Anonymization-TA-GGAD/.

</details>


### [82] [From Representation to Clusters: A Contrastive Learning Approach for Attributed Hypergraph Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09370)
*Li Ni,Shuaikang Zeng,Lin Mu,Longlong Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CAHC，一种端到端的属性超图聚类方法，通过结合节点/超边双层对比学习与联合聚类优化，解决了传统方法缺乏聚类监督的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的属性超图对比学习方法通常先学习嵌入再进行聚类（如k-means），这种两阶段方式缺乏直接的聚类监督，导致学习到的表示可能包含大量与聚类无关的冗余信息。

Method: CAHC包含两个主要阶段：1) 表示学习：利用结合了节点级和超边级目标的对比学习生成嵌入；2) 聚类分配学习：通过聚类导向的联合优化机制同时提炼嵌入并输出聚类结果。

Result: 在8个数据集上的实验结果表明，CAHC在属性超图聚类任务上明显优于现有的基准方法。

Conclusion: CAHC通过端到端的学习范式，有效地解决了对比学习在超图聚类中缺乏直接监督的问题，是处理属性超图聚类任务的一种强有力的新方法。

Abstract: Contrastive learning has demonstrated strong performance in attributed hypergraph clustering. Typically, existing methods based on contrastive learning first learn node embeddings and then apply clustering algorithms, such as k-means, to these embeddings to obtain the clustering results.However, these methods lack direct clustering supervision, risking the inclusion of clustering-irrelevant information in the learned graph.To this end, we propose a Contrastive learning approach for Attributed Hypergraph Clustering (CAHC), an end-to-end method that simultaneously learns node embeddings and obtains clustering results. CAHC consists of two main steps: representation learning and cluster assignment learning. The former employs a novel contrastive learning approach that incorporates both node-level and hyperedge-level objectives to generate node embeddings.The latter joint embedding and clustering optimization to refine these embeddings by clustering-oriented guidance and obtains clustering results simultaneously.Extensive experimental results demonstrate that CAHC outperforms baselines on eight datasets.

</details>


### [83] [SPAARS: Safer RL Policy Alignment through Abstract Exploration and Refined Exploitation of Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09378)
*Swaminathan S K,Aritra Hazra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 SPAARS，一种通过从潜在空间到原始动作空间的课程学习切换，解决离线到在线强化学习中重构损失导致性能瓶颈的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Offline-to-online 强化学习面临安全探索与性能上限的矛盾。现有基于 CVAE 的方法虽然能限制探索范围，但受限于解码器的重构损失，存在“开发差距（exploitation gap）”。

Method: 提出 SPAARS 课程学习框架：第一阶段在 CVAE 的低维潜在空间进行受限探索以保证安全和效率；第二阶段无缝切换至原始动作空间，消除解码器重构带来的性能瓶颈。作者还提供了潜在空间策略梯度的方差减少证明及稳定性分析。

Result: 在 kitchen-mixed-v0 任务中，SPAARS-SUPE 归一化收益（0.825）优于 SUPE（0.75），且样本效率快 5 倍；在 hopper 和 walker2d 任务中，性能大幅超越 IQL 基线，验证了其在无序数据下的有效性。

Conclusion: SPAARS 证明了通过拉格朗日乘子动态调整学习阶段，可以克服生成模型带来的策略上限问题。该方法在不需要轨迹分割的情况下也能取得优异表现，具有极高的实用价值。

Abstract: Offline-to-online reinforcement learning (RL) offers a promising paradigm for robotics by pre-training policies on safe, offline demonstrations and fine-tuning them via online interaction. However, a fundamental challenge remains: how to safely explore online without deviating from the behavioral support of the offline data? While recent methods leverage conditional variational autoencoders (CVAEs) to bound exploration within a latent space, they inherently suffer from an exploitation gap -- a performance ceiling imposed by the decoder's reconstruction loss. We introduce SPAARS, a curriculum learning framework that initially constrains exploration to the low-dimensional latent manifold for sample-efficient, safe behavioral improvement, then seamlessly transfers control to the raw action space, bypassing the decoder bottleneck. SPAARS has two instantiations: the CVAE-based variant requires only unordered (s,a) pairs and no trajectory segmentation; SPAARS-SUPE pairs SPAARS with OPAL temporal skill pretraining for stronger exploration structure at the cost of requiring trajectory chunks. We prove an upper bound on the exploitation gap using the Performance Difference Lemma, establish that latent-space policy gradients achieve provable variance reduction over raw-space exploration, and show that concurrent behavioral cloning during the latent phase directly controls curriculum transition stability. Empirically, SPAARS-SUPE achieves 0.825 normalized return on kitchen-mixed-v0 versus 0.75 for SUPE, with 5x better sample efficiency; standalone SPAARS achieves 92.7 and 102.9 normalized return on hopper-medium-v2 and walker2d-medium-v2 respectively, surpassing IQL baselines of 66.3 and 78.3 respectively, confirming the utility of the unordered-pair CVAE instantiation.

</details>


### [84] [Reconstructing Movement from Sparse Samples: Enhanced Spatio-Temporal Matching Strategies for Low-Frequency Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09412)
*Ali Yousefian,Arianna Burzacchi,Simone Vantini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过优化搜索半径、概率函数及引入移动行为分析，提升了ST匹配算法在处理稀疏GPS数据时的效率与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时空匹配（ST-Matching）算法在路网密集且采样间隔较高（稀疏）的情况下，存在计算效率低和匹配准确度不足的问题。

Method: 提出四项改进：1. 动态缓冲区（Dynamic Buffer）；2. 自适应观测概率（Adaptive Observation Probability）；3. 重新设计的时空评分函数；4. 引入历史移动模式的行为分析。

Result: 在米兰城市路网的实验表明，该改进算法在计算效率和路径质量指标上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 结合动态缓冲区、概率优化和行为分析的改进型ST算法，在缺乏地面真值的情况下也能显著提升离散GPS数据的匹配质量。

Abstract: This paper explores potential improvements to the Spatial-Temporal Matching algorithm for matching the GPS trajectories to road networks. While this algorithm is effective, it presents some limitations in computational efficiency and the accuracy of the results, especially in dense environments with relatively high sampling intervals. To address this, the paper proposes four modifications to the original algorithm: a dynamic buffer, an adaptive observation probability, a redesigned temporal scoring function, and a behavioral analysis to account for the historical mobility patterns. The enhancements are assessed using real-world data from the urban area of Milan, and through newly defined evaluation metrics to be applied in the absence of ground truth. The results of the experiment show significant improvements in performance efficiency and path quality across various metrics.

</details>


### [85] [Impact of Markov Decision Process Design on Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09427)
*Tatjana Krau,Jorge Mandlmaier,Tobias Damm,Frieder Heieck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了 MDP 设计和动力学模型对工业 RL 策略从仿真到实际部署的影响，证明了物理动力学模型能显著提升 50% 的真实环境成功率，并为工业过程控制提供了实践指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管强化学习在工业控制中潜力巨大，但在模拟器中训练的策略在部署到实际硬件时常常面临严重的“sim-to-real”差距。目前缺乏关于 MDP 设计选择如何具体影响这种迁移效果的系统性研究。

Method: 通过一个调色任务（color mixing task），系统性地对比分析了不同的 MDP 配置（状态、目标、奖励、终止准则）以及不同类型的动力学模型（物理驱动型 vs 简化型）在仿真与真实硬件上的表现。

Result: 在严格的精度限制下，基于物理的动力学模型在真实世界中的成功率比简化模型高出 50%，后者在相同环境下完全失效。此外，研究确定了影响策略迁移性能的关键 MDP 设计因素。

Conclusion: 在工业过程控制中，MDP 的设计（特别是状态构成和奖励函数）与环境动力学模型的物理准确性对于弥合 sim-to-real 鸿沟至关重要。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has demonstrated strong potential for industrial process control, yet policies trained in simulation often suffer from a significant sim-to-real gap when deployed on physical hardware. This work systematically analyzes how core Markov Decision Process (MDP) design choices -- state composition, target inclusion, reward formulation, termination criteria, and environment dynamics models -- affect this transfer. Using a color mixing task, we evaluate different MDP configurations and mixing dynamics across simulation and real-world experiments. We validate our findings on physical hardware, demonstrating that physics-based dynamics models achieve up to 50% real-world success under strict precision constraints where simplified models fail entirely. Our results provide practical MDP design guidelines for deploying RL in industrial process control.

</details>


### [86] [From Weighting to Modeling: A Nonparametric Estimator for Off-Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09436)
*Rong J. B. Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非参数加权（NW）及其增强版（MNW）方法，旨在解决离线策略评估中 IPW 导致的高方差问题，实现了更精确的价值估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在上下文多臂老虎机（Contextual Bandits）的离线评估中，传统 IPW 方法常因概率项在分母导致高方差，而 DR 方法虽引入奖励建模但未直接解决 IPW 的方差问题。

Method: 提出非参数加权（NW）法构建权重；并结合奖励预测模型提出模型辅助非参数加权（MNW）法，通过显式建模来减轻奖励模型的偏差。

Result: 广泛的实证对比表明，所提方法在保持低偏差的同时，显著降低了价值估计的方差，表现优于现有技术。

Conclusion: NW 和 MNW 在减少方差的同时保持了低偏差，是比传统 IPW 和 DR 更稳健的离线策略评估工具。

Abstract: We study off-policy evaluation in the setting of contextual bandits, where we aim to evaluate a new policy using historical data that consists of contexts, actions and received rewards. This historical data typically does not faithfully represent action distribution of the new policy accurately. A common approach, inverse probability weighting (IPW), adjusts for these discrepancies in action distributions. However, this method often suffers from high variance due to the probability being in the denominator. The doubly robust (DR) estimator reduces variance through modeling reward but does not directly address variance from IPW. In this work, we address the limitation of IPW by proposing a Nonparametric Weighting (NW) approach that constructs weights using a nonparametric model. Our NW approach achieves low bias like IPW but typically exhibits significantly lower variance. To further reduce variance, we incorporate reward predictions -- similar to the DR technique -- resulting in the Model-assisted Nonparametric Weighting (MNW) approach. The MNW approach yields accurate value estimates by explicitly modeling and mitigating bias from reward modeling, without aiming to guarantee the standard doubly robust property. Extensive empirical comparisons show that our approaches consistently outperform existing techniques, achieving lower variance in value estimation while maintaining low bias.

</details>


### [87] [Temporal-Conditioned Normalizing Flows for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09490)
*David Baumgartner,Helge Langseth,Kenth Engø-Monsen,Heri Ramampiaro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 tcNF 框架，通过引入时间条件的自回归归一化流，实现对时间序列分布的精确建模，从而高效识别低概率异常事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对传统异常检测方法在处理时间序列时，难以同时精确建模复杂的时序依赖关系和评估不确定性的问题。

Method: 提出时间条件归一化流（tcNF），将归一化流与自回归机制结合，通过对历史观测值的条件建模来捕捉复杂的时序动态。

Result: 在多个不同类型的数据集上表现优异，相比现有方法在准确性和鲁棒性方面均有显著提升。

Conclusion: tcNF 为时间序列异常检测提供了一个稳健的框架，通过精确的概率建模和不确定性量化，有效提升了检测精度。

Abstract: This paper introduces temporal-conditioned normalizing flows (tcNF), a novel framework that addresses anomaly detection in time series data with accurate modeling of temporal dependencies and uncertainty. By conditioning normalizing flows on previous observations, tcNF effectively captures complex temporal dynamics and generates accurate probability distributions of expected behavior. This autoregressive approach enables robust anomaly detection by identifying low-probability events within the learned distribution. We evaluate tcNF on diverse datasets, demonstrating good accuracy and robustness compared to existing methods. A comprehensive analysis of strengths and limitations and open-source code is provided to facilitate reproducibility and future research.

</details>


### [88] [Efficiently Aligning Draft Models via Parameter- and Data-Efficient Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09527)
*Luxi Lin,Zhihang Lin,Zhanpeng Zeng,Yuhao Chen,Qingyu Zhang,Jixiang Luo,Xuelong Li,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 EDA 框架，通过参数高效的解耦架构、数据再生和样本选择机制，低成本地为微调后的目标模型适配草图模型，从而提升推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当目标大模型针对特定领域进行微调后，现有的投机解码由于草图模型未同步适配而导致加速性能下降，而为每个目标模型重新训练草图模型成本过高。

Method: 提出 EDA 框架，包含三个创新点：(1) 解耦架构：使用共享和私有组件分别建模输出分布，仅更新轻量化私有组件；(2) 数据再生：利用微调后的目标模型重新生成数据以增强对齐；(3) 样本选择：优先处理高价值数据以提升适配效率。

Result: 实验表明，EDA 在减少训练成本的同时，有效恢复了微调模型的投机采样性能，并获得了更高的平均接受长度。

Conclusion: EDA 为特定领域的投机采样提供了一种高性价比的解决方案，在显著降低训练成本的同时，有效恢复并提升了模型推理的加速效果。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference but suffers from performance degradation when target models are fine-tuned for specific domains. A naive solution is to retrain draft models for every target model, which is costly and inefficient. To address this, we introduce a parameter- and data-efficient framework named Efficient Draft Adaptation, abbreviated as EDA, for efficiently adapting draft models. EDA introduces three innovations: (1) a decoupled architecture that utilizes shared and private components to model the shared and target-specific output distributions separately, enabling parameter-efficient adaptation by updating only the lightweight private component;(2) a data regeneration strategy that utilizes the fine-tuned target model to regenerate training data, thereby improving the alignment between training and speculative decoding, leading to higher average acceptance length;(3) a sample selection mechanism that prioritizes high-value data for efficient adaptation. Our experiments show that EDA effectively restores speculative performance on fine-tuned models, achieving superior average acceptance lengths with significantly reduced training costs compared to full retraining. Code is available at https://github.com/Lyn-Lucy/Efficient-Draft-Adaptation.

</details>


### [89] [An Optimal Control Approach To Transformer Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09571)
*Kağan Akman,Naci Saldı,Serdar Yüksel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于最优控制理论的 Transformer 训练新方法，通过将模型映射到概率测度空间并利用动态规划和三重量化技术，实现了无需梯度且具有全局最优保证的稳健训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Transformer 训练缺乏严谨的控制论框架，难以同时处理执行时的输入独立性、系综控制性质及位置依赖性，且梯度下降法在非光滑/非凸场景下存在局限。

Method: 将 Transformer 建模为具有共享动作的离散时间控制粒子系统（McKean-Vlasov 动力学），通过外推至概率测度空间（Lifting）构建马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）；随后采用状态、测度及动作空间的“三重量化”训练程序。

Result: 证明了在提升空间（Lifted space）中闭环策略与初始分布相关的开环策略等价，并证明了三重量化模型的全局最优策略是原始问题的近优解。

Conclusion: 该模型在初始经验测度扰动下具有稳定性，且随着数据量增加表现出策略收敛性。这为非光滑、非凸环境下的 Transformer 训练提供了全局最优且稳健的替代方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a rigorous optimal control-theoretic approach to Transformer training that respects key structural constraints such as (i) realized-input-independence during execution, (ii) the ensemble control nature of the problem, and (iii) positional dependence. We model the Transformer architecture as a discrete-time controlled particle system with shared actions, exhibiting noise-free McKean-Vlasov dynamics. While the resulting dynamics is not Markovian, we show that lifting it to probability measures produces a fully-observed Markov decision process (MDP). Positional encodings are incorporated into the state space to preserve the sequence order under lifting.
  Using the dynamic programming principle, we establish the existence of globally optimal policies under mild assumptions of compactness. We further prove that closed-loop policies in the lifted is equivalent to an initial-distribution dependent open-loop policy, which are realized-input-independent and compatible with standard Transformer training.
  To train a Transformer, we propose a triply quantized training procedure for the lifted MDP by quantizing the state space, the space of probability measures, and the action space, and show that any optimal policy for the triply quantized model is near-optimal for the original training problem.
  Finally, we establish stability and empirical consistency properties of the lifted model by showing that the value function is continuous with respect to the perturbations of the initial empirical measures and convergence of policies as the data size increases. This approach provides a globally optimal and robust alternative to gradient-based training without requiring smoothness or convexity.

</details>


### [90] [Towards Understanding Adam Convergence on Highly Degenerate Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09581)
*Zhiwei Bai,Jiajie Zhao,Zhangchen Zhou,Zhi-Qin John Xu,Yaoyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了 Adam 在退化多项式函数上无需学习率调度即可实现线性收敛，显著快于梯度下降，并揭示了其超参数行为相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 Adam 被广泛应用，但其具有固有优势的具体目标函数类别尚不明朗，且以往研究多依赖外部调度器或特定的 $\beta_2$ 设置。本研究旨在探索 Adam 的“自然”自动收敛属性。

Method: 通过推导退化多项式的局部渐近稳定性理论条件，结合数值实验验证，并分析二阶动量 $v_t$ 与梯度平方 $g_t^2$ 的解耦机制。

Result: 证明了 Adam 在退化函数上可实现局部线性收敛（优于 GD 的次线性收敛）；揭示了 $v_t$ 与 $g_t^2$ 的解耦能指数级放大有效学习率；并刻画了包含稳定收敛、尖峰和类 SignGD 振荡三个区域的超参数相图。

Conclusion: Adam 在高度退化的多项式函数上具有天然的自动收敛优势，其收敛速度本质上优于 GD 和 Momentum。研究还通过超参数相图揭示了其行为边界。

Abstract: Adam is a widely used optimization algorithm in deep learning, yet the specific class of objective functions where it exhibits inherent advantages remains underexplored. Unlike prior studies requiring external schedulers and $β_2$ near 1 for convergence, this work investigates the "natural" auto-convergence properties of Adam. We identify a class of highly degenerate polynomials where Adam converges automatically without additional schedulers. Specifically, we derive theoretical conditions for local asymptotic stability on degenerate polynomials and demonstrate strong alignment between theoretical bounds and experimental results. We prove that Adam achieves local linear convergence on these degenerate functions, significantly outperforming the sub-linear convergence of Gradient Descent and Momentum. This acceleration stems from a decoupling mechanism between the second moment $v_t$ and squared gradient $g_t^2$, which exponentially amplifies the effective learning rate. Finally, we characterize Adam's hyperparameter phase diagram, identifying three distinct behavioral regimes: stable convergence, spikes, and SignGD-like oscillation.

</details>


### [91] [Nonparametric Variational Differential Privacy via Embedding Parameter Clipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09583)
*Dina El Zein,Shashi Kumar,James Henderson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入基于数学推导的参数裁剪策略，解决了非参数变分隐私模型的表示漂移和数值不稳定问题，显著提升了模型的隐私保护能力和任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非参数变分信息瓶颈（NVIB）在构建隐私保护语言模型时，学习到的潜在表示容易漂移至高信息区域，导致隐私保护能力下降，并因训练中的数值不稳定而降低实用性。

Method: 提出了一种基于数学推导的参数裁剪策略。该策略通过最小化 Rényi 散度（RD）上界，对后验均值、方差和混合权重参数施加理论依据的约束，以防止潜在表示漂移到高信息含量区域。

Result: 与未受约束的基准模型相比，应用参数裁剪的模型在 Rényi 散度上界上表现更紧致（意味着隐私更强），同时在多个下游任务中取得了更高的性能。

Conclusion: 参数裁剪是改善非参数变分模型中隐私与效能权衡的一种简单且有效的方法，增强了此类模型在实际应用中的鲁棒性。

Abstract: The nonparametric variational information bottleneck (NVIB) provides the foundation for nonparametric variational differential privacy (NVDP), a framework for building privacy-preserving language models. However, the learned latent representations can drift into regions with high information content, leading to poor privacy guarantees, but also low utility due to numerical instability during training. In this work, we introduce a principled parameter clipping strategy to directly address this issue. Our method is mathematically derived from the objective of minimizing the Rényi Divergence (RD) upper bound, yielding specific, theoretically grounded constraints on the posterior mean, variance, and mixture weight parameters. We apply our technique to an NVIB based model and empirically compare it against an unconstrained baseline. Our findings demonstrate that the clipped model consistently achieves tighter RD bounds, implying stronger privacy, while simultaneously attaining higher performance on several downstream tasks. This work presents a simple yet effective method for improving the privacy-utility trade-off in variational models, making them more robust and practical.

</details>


### [92] [GNNs for Time Series Anomaly Detection: An Open-Source Framework and a Critical Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09675)
*Federico Bello,Gonzalo Chiarlone,Marcelo Fiori,Gastón García González,Federico Larroca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于GNN时间序列异常检测的开源框架，旨在解决评估标准不一的问题；通过实验证明了GNN在提升检测性能的同时具备显著的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管GNN在时间序列异常检测中展现了潜力，但由于缺乏标准化评估框架，且在指标设计和结果解释方面存在持续性问题，制约了该领域的发展。

Method: 开发了一个开源的、灵活的TSAD实验框架。在该框架下评估了多种GNN架构（如基于注意力机制的GNN）及基准模型，并在具有不同结构特性的两个真实数据集上进行系统对比。

Result: GNN在性能和可解释性上均优于基准；当图结构不确定时，基于吸引力的GNN表现更稳健。此外，揭示了当前某些评估指标和阈值策略可能会掩盖真实的模型对比效果。

Conclusion: GNN不仅提升了异常检测性能，还提供了显著的可解释性优势。研究还通过开源框架为该领域提供了标准化的实验工具。

Abstract: There is growing interest in applying graph-based methods to Time Series Anomaly Detection (TSAD), particularly Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), as they naturally model dependencies among multivariate signals. GNNs are typically used as backbones in score-based TSAD pipelines, where anomalies are identified through reconstruction or prediction errors followed by thresholding. However, and despite promising results, the field still lacks standardized frameworks for evaluation and suffers from persistent issues with metric design and interpretation. We thus present an open-source framework for TSAD using GNNs, designed to support reproducible experimentation across datasets, graph structures, and evaluation strategies. Built with flexibility and extensibility in mind, the framework facilitates systematic comparisons between TSAD models and enables in-depth analysis of performance and interpretability. Using this tool, we evaluate several GNN-based architectures alongside baseline models across two real-world datasets with contrasting structural characteristics. Our results show that GNNs not only improve detection performance but also offer significant gains in interpretability, an especially valuable feature for practical diagnosis. We also find that attention-based GNNs offer robustness when graph structure is uncertain or inferred. In addition, we reflect on common evaluation practices in TSAD, showing how certain metrics and thresholding strategies can obscure meaningful comparisons. Overall, this work contributes both practical tools and critical insights to advance the development and evaluation of graph-based TSAD systems.

</details>


### [93] [FreqCycle: A Multi-Scale Time-Frequency Analysis Method for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09661)
*Boya Zhang,Shuaijie Yin,Huiwen Zhu,Xing He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 核心是通过 FECF 和 SFPL 模块协同提取全频段特征，并利用层次化架构解决多周期耦合问题，实现高效精准的时间序列预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注能量集中的低频模式，忽视了中高频特征，限制了深度学习模型在时间序列预测中的性能提升。

Method: 提出 FreqCycle 框架，包含提取低频周期模式的 FECF 模块和增强中高频能量特征的 SFPL 模块，并进一步扩展为层次化架构 MFreqCycle 以处理多周期耦合。

Result: 在七个不同领域的基准数据集上，该模型在预测精度上达到最优，并具备更快的推理速度。

Conclusion: FreqCycle 在保持高效推理速度的同时，成功突破了深度学习模型在时间序列中高频信息建模的局限性，达到了 SOTA 性能。

Abstract: Mining time-frequency features is critical for time series forecasting. Existing research has predominantly focused on modeling low-frequency patterns, where most time series energy is concentrated. The overlooking of mid to high frequency continues to limit further performance gains in deep learning models. We propose FreqCycle, a novel framework integrating: (i) a Filter-Enhanced Cycle Forecasting (FECF) module to extract low-frequency features by explicitly learning shared periodic patterns in the time domain, and (ii) a Segmented Frequency-domain Pattern Learning (SFPL) module to enhance mid to high frequency energy proportion via learnable filters and adaptive weighting. Furthermore, time series data often exhibit coupled multi-periodicity, such as intertwined weekly and daily cycles. To address coupled multi-periodicity as well as long lookback window challenges, we extend FreqCycle hierarchically into MFreqCycle, which decouples nested periodic features through cross-scale interactions. Extensive experiments on seven diverse domain benchmarks demonstrate that FreqCycle achieves state-of-the-art accuracy while maintaining faster inference speeds, striking an optimal balance between performance and efficiency.

</details>


### [94] [No evaluation without fair representation : Impact of label and selection bias on the evaluation, performance and mitigation of classification models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09662)
*Magali Legast,Toon Calders,François Fouss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过新框架分析了标签和选择偏置对机器学习公平性的不同影响，揭示了在公正评估环境下准确性与公平性可兼得，并强调了偏置类型对缓解策略效果的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习中的不同偏置类型（如选择偏置、标签偏置）对公平性的影响各异，但其具体差异和对偏差缓解方法有效性的影响尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 提出了一个新的偏置与评估框架，通过在低歧视的真实数据集中引入受控的偏置，独立模拟并分析标签偏置和多种选择偏置子类对模型及缓解法的影响。

Result: 实验表明，不同类型的偏置对模型表现有显著不同的影响；在无偏测试集上评估时，并未发现公平性与准确性、个人公平与群体公平之间的冲突；缓解方法的有效性高度依赖于偏置类型。

Conclusion: 在公正的测试集上，公平性与准确性之间并不存在必然的权衡关系。未来研究应致力于开发更准确的评估方法，并深入探讨多种偏差组合及数据集特征对缓解策略的影响。

Abstract: Bias can be introduced in diverse ways in machine learning datasets, for example via selection or label bias. Although these bias types in themselves have an influence on important aspects of fair machine learning, their different impact has been understudied. In this work, we empirically analyze the effect of label bias and several subtypes of selection bias on the evaluation of classification models, on their performance, and on the effectiveness of bias mitigation methods. We also introduce a biasing and evaluation framework that allows to model fair worlds and their biased counterparts through the introduction of controlled bias in real-life datasets with low discrimination. Using our framework, we empirically analyze the impact of each bias type independently, while obtaining a more representative evaluation of models and mitigation methods than with the traditional use of a subset of biased data as test set. Our results highlight different factors that influence how impactful bias is on model performance. They also show an absence of trade-off between fairness and accuracy, and between individual and group fairness, when models are evaluated on a test set that does not exhibit unwanted bias. They furthermore indicate that the performance of bias mitigation methods is influenced by the type of bias present in the data. Our findings call for future work to develop more accurate evaluations of prediction models and fairness interventions, but also to better understand other types of bias, more complex scenarios involving the combination of different bias types, and other factors that impact the efficiency of the mitigation methods, such as dataset characteristics.

</details>


### [95] [On Catastrophic Forgetting in Low-Rank Decomposition-Based Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09684)
*Muhammad Ahmad,Jingjing Zheng,Yankai Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了 PEFT 方法在顺序学习中的遗忘问题，发现更新子空间的几何结构和参数化方式是缓解灾难性遗忘的关键，并指出张量分解与结构对齐方法更具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然 LoRA 等参数高效微调（PEFT）方法已广泛应用，但其在顺序学习（Sequential Learning）中的表现，特别是针对“灾难性遗忘”的机制，尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 通过实证研究，对比了不同低秩分解方法（如 LoRA、基于张量的 LoRETTA、结构对齐的 WeGeFT）在顺序学习任务中的表现，分析了其几何属性与更新子空间对遗忘的影响。

Result: 限制在小型共享矩阵子空间的更新易导致任务干扰；而张量分解（如 LoRETTA）能以极小预算捕捉丰富结构信息以减轻遗忘；结构对齐方法（如 WeGeFT）则能更好地保留预训练表示。

Conclusion: 更新子空间的设计是持续学习中的关键因素。选择具有丰富结构化信息或结构对齐特征的参数化方法，是提升顺序学习鲁棒性的关键。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) based on low-rank decomposition, such as LoRA, has become a standard for adapting large pretrained models. However, its behavior in sequential learning -- specifically regarding catastrophic forgetting -- remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we present an empirical study showing that forgetting is strongly influenced by the geometry and parameterization of the update subspace. While methods that restrict updates to small, shared matrix subspaces often suffer from task interference, tensor-based decompositions (e.g., LoRETTA) mitigate forgetting by capturing richer structural information within ultra-compact budgets, and structurally aligned parameterizations (e.g., WeGeFT) preserve pretrained representations. Our findings highlight update subspace design as a key factor in continual learning and offer practical guidance for selecting efficient adaptation strategies in sequential settings.

</details>


### [96] [A Hybrid Quantum-Classical Framework for Financial Volatility Forecasting Based on Quantum Circuit Born Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09789)
*Yixiong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种结合LSTM与量子电路波恩机（QCBM）的混合模型，显著提升了金融市场波动率预测的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计量模型和经典机器学习难以处理金融时间序列固有的非线性与非平稳特性，需探索利用量子计算的优势来提升复杂分布的建模能力。

Method: 提出一种混合量子-经典计算框架：利用LSTM提取历史时间序列的动态特征，并结合量子电路波恩机（QCBM）作为可学习的先验模块，为预测过程提供高质量的先验分布。

Result: 在上海证券交易所综合指数和沪深300指数的高频数据实验中，该模型在MSE、RMSE和QLIKE等指标上均优于纯经典的LSTM基准模型。

Conclusion: 量子增强的混合架构在处理高维、非线性金融预测任务中具有巨大潜力，且该框架具备扩展到其他机器学习领域的灵活性。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of financial market volatility is crucial for risk management, option pricing, and portfolio optimization. Traditional econometric models and classical machine learning methods face challenges in handling the inherent non-linear and non-stationary characteristics of financial time series. In recent years, the rapid development of quantum computing has provided a new paradigm for solving complex optimization and sampling problems. This paper proposes a novel hybrid quantum-classical computing framework aimed at combining the powerful representation capabilities of classical neural networks with the unique advantages of quantum models. For the specific task of financial market volatility forecasting, we designed and implemented a hybrid model based on this framework, which combines a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) network with a Quantum Circuit Born Machine (QCBM). The LSTM is responsible for extracting complex dynamic features from historical time series data, while the QCBM serves as a learnable prior module, providing the model with a high-quality prior distribution to guide the forecasting process. We evaluated the model on two real financial datasets consisting of 5-minute high-frequency data from the Shanghai Stock Exchange (SSE) Composite Index and CSI 300 Index. Experimental results show that, compared to a purely classical LSTM baseline model, our hybrid quantum-classical model demonstrates significant advantages across multiple key metrics, including Mean Squared Error (MSE), Root Mean Squared Error (RMSE), and QLIKE loss, proving the great potential of quantum computing in enhancing the capabilities of financial forecasting models. More broadly, the proposed hybrid framework offers a flexible architecture that may be adapted to other machine learning tasks involving high-dimensional, complex, or non-linear data distributions.

</details>


### [97] [Exploiting Label-Aware Channel Scoring for Adaptive Channel Pruning in Split Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09792)
*Jialei Tan,Zheng Lin,Xiangming Cai,Ruoxi Zhu,Zihan Fang,Pingping Chen,Wei Ni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 ACP-SL 方案，通过自适应通道剪枝技术压缩中间特征数据，显著降低了分裂学习中的通信开销并提升了模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分裂学习（SL）中，客户端与服务器之间传输的中间特征（Smashed data）会产生巨大的通信开销，尤其在多客户端场景下限制了系统效率。

Method: 提出 ACP-SL 方案，包含两个核心模块：1) 标签感知通道重要性评分（LCIS）模块，用于评估通道贡献度；2) 自适应通道剪枝（ACP）模块，基于评分剪掉次要通道。

Result: 实验表明，ACP-SL 在测试准确率上优于基准方案，且达到目标准确率所需的训练轮数更少，显著降低了通信成本。

Conclusion: ACP-SL 能够有效平衡计算负载与通信开销，是处理大规模客户端分裂学习任务的高效方案。

Abstract: Split learning (SL) transfers most of the training workload to the server, which alleviates computational burden on client devices. However, the transmission of intermediate feature representations, referred to as smashed data, incurs significant communication overhead, particularly when a large number of client devices are involved. To address this challenge, we propose an adaptive channel pruning-aided SL (ACP-SL) scheme. In ACP-SL, a label-aware channel importance scoring (LCIS) module is designed to generate channel importance scores, distinguishing important channels from less important ones. Based on these scores, an adaptive channel pruning (ACP) module is developed to prune less important channels, thereby compressing the corresponding smashed data and reducing the communication overhead. Experimental results show that ACP-SL consistently outperforms benchmark schemes in test accuracy. Furthermore, it reaches a target test accuracy in fewer training rounds, thereby reducing communication overhead.

</details>


### [98] [A Multi-Prototype-Guided Federated Knowledge Distillation Approach in AI-RAN Enabled Multi-Access Edge Computing System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09727)
*Luyao Zou,Hayoung Oh,Chu Myaet Thwal,Apurba Adhikary,Seohyeon Hong,Zhu Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对边缘计算联邦学习中的数据异构问题，提出了基于多原型引导的联邦知识蒸馏（MP-FedKD）方法，通过聚类提取细粒度特征并优化损失函数，显著提升了模型在非独立同分布数据下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI-RAN赋能的边缘计算（MEC）系统中，联邦学习面临严重的非独立同分布（non-IID）数据挑战。现有基于单原型的方案因平均化操作会导致关键信息丢失。

Method: 提出 MP-FedKD 方法。核心包括：1. 集成自知识蒸馏；2. 采用多原型策略，通过条件层次聚类（CHAC）和原型对齐方案提取更丰富的特征信息；3. 设计 LEMGP 损失函数来优化全局原型与本地嵌入的关系。

Result: 在多个数据集及不同non-IID设置下的实验表明，MP-FedKD 在准确率、平均准确度及误差指标（RMSE、MAE）上均优于现有的最先进基准方法。

Conclusion: MP-FedKD是一种在大规模AI-RAN边缘计算系统中处理非独立同分布（non-IID）数据的有效方案，通过多原型策略显著提升了联邦学习的泛化能力。

Abstract: With the development of wireless network, Multi-Access Edge Computing (MEC) and Artificial Intelligence (AI)-native Radio Access Network (RAN) have attracted significant attention. Particularly, the integration of AI-RAN and MEC is envisioned to transform network efficiency and responsiveness. Therefore, it is valuable to investigate AI-RAN enabled MEC system. Federated learning (FL) nowadays is emerging as a promising approach for AI-RAN enabled MEC system, in which edge devices are enabled to train a global model cooperatively without revealing their raw data. However, conventional FL encounters the challenge in processing the non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) data. Single prototype obtained by averaging the embedding vectors per class can be employed in FL to handle the data heterogeneity issue. Nevertheless, this may result in the loss of useful information owing to the average operation. Therefore, in this paper, a multi-prototype-guided federated knowledge distillation (MP-FedKD) approach is proposed. Particularly, self-knowledge distillation is integrated into FL to deal with the non-IID issue. To cope with the problem of information loss caused by single prototype-based strategy, multi-prototype strategy is adopted, where we present a conditional hierarchical agglomerative clustering (CHAC) approach and a prototype alignment scheme. Additionally, we design a novel loss function (called LEMGP loss) for each local client, where the relationship between global prototypes and local embedding will be focused. Extensive experiments over multiple datasets with various non-IID settings showcase that the proposed MP-FedKD approach outperforms the considered state-of-the-art baselines regarding accuracy, average accuracy and errors (RMSE and MAE).

</details>


### [99] [Towards a Neural Debugger for Python](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09951)
*Maximilian Beck,Jonas Gehring,Jannik Kossen,Gabriel Synnaeve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文引入了“神经调试器”，使大语言模型能够像传统调试器一样交互式地模拟 Python 代码执行过程，支持断点和单步调试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经解释器（Neural Interpreters）虽然能预测程序逐行执行，但缺乏交互控制，无法像开发者使用调试器那样灵活地在特定位置停止或检查变量。

Method: 通过微调大型语言模型（LLMs）或从头训练小模型，使其能够模拟传统调试器的操作（如单步执行 step into/over/out、设置断点），并支持正向状态预测和逆向输入推理。

Result: 在 CruxEval 基准测试中，该模型在输出预测（正向）和输入预测（逆向）任务上均表现出色，证明了其强大的条件执行建模能力。

Conclusion: 神经调试器为未来的人机交互编程和智能体提供了“世界模型”，为代码生成、程序理解和自动化调试奠定了基础。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) on Python execution traces grounds them in code execution and enables the line-by-line execution prediction of whole Python programs, effectively turning them into neural interpreters (FAIR CodeGen Team et al., 2025). However, developers rarely execute programs step by step; instead, they use debuggers to stop execution at certain breakpoints and step through relevant portions only while inspecting or modifying program variables. Existing neural interpreter approaches lack such interactive control. To address this limitation, we introduce neural debuggers: language models that emulate traditional debuggers, supporting operations such as stepping into, over, or out of functions, as well as setting breakpoints at specific source lines. We show that neural debuggers -- obtained via fine-tuning large LLMs or pre-training smaller models from scratch -- can reliably model both forward execution (predicting future states and outputs) and inverse execution (inferring prior states or inputs) conditioned on debugger actions. Evaluated on CruxEval, our models achieve strong performance on both output and input prediction tasks, demonstrating robust conditional execution modeling. Our work takes first steps towards future agentic coding systems in which neural debuggers serve as a world model for simulated debugging environments, providing execution feedback or enabling agents to interact with real debugging tools. This capability lays the foundation for more powerful code generation, program understanding, and automated debugging.

</details>


### [100] [Information Theoretic Bayesian Optimization over the Probability Simplex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09793)
*Federico Pavesi,Antonio Candelieri,Noémie Jaquier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 $\alpha$-GaBO，通过引入信息几何理论，为概率单纯形约束下的贝叶斯优化设计了专用的核函数和优化算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多应用涉及概率和混合物的优化（其域为概率单纯形），传统的欧几里得贝叶斯优化难以有效处理这种非负且和为一的非欧几里得约束空间。

Method: 提出 $\alpha$-GaBO 算法家族，利用信息几何（黎曼几何分支）为单纯形赋予度量和联络，构建了符合其几何特性的 Matérn 核，并设计了参数化的几何优化器用于采集函数。

Result: 在基准函数、混合分类器、组件混合以及机器人控制任务中验证了有效性，表现出比现有约束欧式贝叶斯优化更高的性能。

Conclusion: 结合信息几何的博弈与优化方法，在概率单纯形约束下的黑盒优化问题上显著优于传统的约束欧几里得方法。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization is a data-efficient technique that has been shown to be extremely powerful to optimize expensive, black-box, and possibly noisy objective functions. Many applications involve optimizing probabilities and mixtures which naturally belong to the probability simplex, a constrained non-Euclidean domain defined by non-negative entries summing to one. This paper introduces $α$-GaBO, a novel family of Bayesian optimization algorithms over the probability simplex. Our approach is grounded in information geometry, a branch of Riemannian geometry which endows the simplex with a Riemannian metric and a class of connections. Based on information geometry theory, we construct Matérn kernels that reflect the geometry of the probability simplex, as well as a one-parameter family of geometric optimizers for the acquisition function. We validate our method on benchmark functions and on a variety of real-world applications including mixtures of components, mixtures of classifiers, and a robotic control task, showing its increased performance compared to constrained Euclidean approaches.

</details>


### [101] [Good Reasoning Makes Good Demonstrations: Implicit Reasoning Quality Supervision via In-Context Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09803)
*Tiehua Mei,Minxuan Lv,Leiyu Pan,Zhenpeng Su,Hongru Hou,Hengrui Chen,Ao Xu,Deqing Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 In-Context RLVR，利用模型自身的上下文学习能力来区分正确答案背后的推理质量，通过对高质量路径分配更高权重，提升了 LLM 在数学推理任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的带验证器强化学习（RLVR）在推理任务中将所有得到正确答案的路径等同对待，这可能导致模型强化那些“歪打正着”的错误推理逻辑。

Method: 引入“证据增益（Evidence Gain）”指称，利用策略模型自身的“上下文学习（ICL）”能力来衡量推理轨迹作为示例的效用。通过贝叶斯分析，设计出 In-Context RLVR 目标函数，在训练中根据证据增益对奖励进行隐式重加权。

Result: 在数学基准测试中，相比于标准 RLVR，In-Context RLVR 不仅提高了模型回答的准确率，还显著提升了推理过程的质量，且无需昂贵的外部评估成本。

Conclusion: IC-RLVR 证明了模型可以通过评估自身的“教学能力”来识别更高质量的推理路径，从而在无需外部额外标注的情况下实现更优的强化学习效果。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) improves reasoning in large language models but treats all correct solutions equally, potentially reinforcing flawed traces that get correct answers by chance. We observe that better reasoning are better teachers: high-quality solutions serve as more effective demonstrations than low-quality ones. We term this teaching ability Demonstration Utility, and show that the policy model's own in-context learning ability provides an efficient way to measure it, yielding a quality signal termed Evidence Gain. To employ this signal during training, we introduce In-Context RLVR. By Bayesian analysis, we show that this objective implicitly reweights rewards by Evidence Gain, assigning higher weights to high-quality traces and lower weights to low-quality ones, without requiring costly computation or external evaluators. Experiments on mathematical benchmarks show improvements in both accuracy and reasoning quality over standard RLVR.

</details>


### [102] [GAST: Gradient-aligned Sparse Tuning of Large Language Models with Data-layer Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09865)
*Kai Yao,Zhenghan Song,Kaixin Wu,Mingjie Zhong,Danzhao Cheng,Zhaorui Tan,Yixin Ji,Penglei Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GAST 是一种新型 PEFT 方法，它打破了层选择与数据选择的界限，通过自适应地为模型不同层匹配最有效的数据子集，实现了更高效、更精准的模型微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 PEFT 方法通常孤立地进行“层选择”或“数据选择”，忽略了不同数据对模型不同层贡献度不同的事实，且容易丢弃低质量数据中的潜在价值。

Method: 提出 GAST（梯度对齐稀疏微调）方法，通过层稀疏策略，为每个模型层自适应地选择最具影响力的训练数据，实现数据与层的联合优化。

Result: 实验结果显示，GAST 在多项基准测试中持续优于现有的基准方法，在降低开销的同时提升了模型表现。

Conclusion: GAST 为参数高效微调（PEFT）提供了一个更有前景的方向，证明了统一优化数据和层选择比单一维度优化更有效。

Abstract: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) has become a key strategy for adapting large language models, with recent advances in sparse tuning reducing overhead by selectively updating key parameters or subsets of data. Existing approaches generally focus on two distinct paradigms: layer-selective methods aiming to fine-tune critical layers to minimize computational load, and data-selective methods aiming to select effective training subsets to boost training. However, current methods typically overlook the fact that different data points contribute varying degrees to distinct model layers, and they often discard potentially valuable information from data perceived as of low quality. To address these limitations, we propose Gradient-aligned Sparse Tuning (GAST), an innovative method that simultaneously performs selective fine-tuning at both data and layer dimensions as integral components of a unified optimization strategy. GAST specifically targets redundancy in information by employing a layer-sparse strategy that adaptively selects the most impactful data points for each layer, providing a more comprehensive and sophisticated solution than approaches restricted to a single dimension. Experiments demonstrate that GAST consistently outperforms baseline methods, establishing a promising direction for future research in PEFT strategies.

</details>


### [103] [CarbonBench: A Global Benchmark for Upscaling of Carbon Fluxes Using Zero-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09868)
*Aleksei Rozanov,Arvind Renganathan,Yimeng Zhang,Vipin Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CarbonBench 是首个针对全球碳通量上采样设计的零样本空间迁移基准，旨在解决模型在未知气候和植被类型下的泛化难题，推动地球系统科学与机器学习的融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的陆地碳交换量化模型在面对观测稀疏、未见过的生态系统时泛化能力不足，且缺乏标准化的基准来严格评估模型跨地理区域和气候带的迁移性能。

Method: 构建了一个包含全球 567 个通量塔、130 万条日观测数据的基准库。提供了分层评估方案（测试未见过的植被和气候类型）、统一的遥感与气象特征集，并建立了从传统树模型到领域泛化架构的多类基准模型。

Result: 推出了首个用于碳通量提升（upscaling）的零样本空间迁移基准 CarbonBench，实现了空间迁移与时间自相关的分离评估，能有效检验模型在分布偏移下的回归能力。

Conclusion: CarbonBench 填补了碳通量量化领域缺乏标准化零样本空间迁移基准的空白，不仅能系统评估模型在异构气候和植被下的表现，还为开发更具鲁棒性的全球气候模型奠定了基础。

Abstract: Accurately quantifying terrestrial carbon exchange is essential for climate policy and carbon accounting, yet models must generalize to ecosystems underrepresented in sparse eddy covariance observations. Despite this challenge being a natural instance of zero-shot spatial transfer learning for time series regression, no standardized benchmark exists to rigorously evaluate model performance across geographically distinct locations with different climate regimes and vegetation types.
  We introduce CarbonBench, the first benchmark for zero-shot spatial transfer in carbon flux upscaling. CarbonBench comprises over 1.3 million daily observations from 567 flux tower sites globally (2000-2024). It provides: (1) stratified evaluation protocols that explicitly test generalization across unseen vegetation types and climate regimes, separating spatial transfer from temporal autocorrelation; (2) a harmonized set of remote sensing and meteorological features to enable flexible architecture design; and (3) baselines ranging from tree-based methods to domain-generalization architectures. By bridging machine learning methodologies and Earth system science, CarbonBench aims to enable systematic comparison of transfer learning methods, serves as a testbed for regression under distribution shift, and contributes to the next-generation climate modeling efforts.

</details>


### [104] [OptEMA: Adaptive Exponential Moving Average for Stochastic Optimization with Zero-Noise Optimality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09923)
*Ganzhao Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 OptEMA 优化框架，通过自适应 EMA 系数实现了无需 Lipschitz 常数且能根据梯度噪声自动切换收敛速度的优化方法，解决了 Adam 类算法在零噪声下表现不佳及参数依赖等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 Adam 等主流优化器在零噪声环境下性能次优、依赖有界梯度等严格假设、以及步长设置非闭环且需预知 Lipschitz 常数等局限性，寻求一种更稳健且自适应的优化方案。

Method: 提出了 OptEMA 算法及其两个变体（OptEMA-M 和 OptEMA-V），通过应用自适应且递减的指数移动平均（EMA）系数，使其步长能够根据运行轨迹自动调整，且无需预知 Lipschitz 常数。

Result: 在标准 SGD 假设下，OptEMA 实现了 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/2} + \sigma^{1/2} T^{-1/4})$ 的噪声自适应收敛率。在零噪声（$\sigma=0$）时，能自动达到 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/2})$ 的近乎最优确定性收敛率。

Conclusion: OptEMA 及其变体显著改进了 Adam 类优化器的性能边界，减少了对超参数手动调整的依赖，并为 Lipschitz 自由和闭环优化算法的研究提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: The Exponential Moving Average (EMA) is a cornerstone of widely used optimizers such as Adam. However, existing theoretical analyses of Adam-style methods have notable limitations: their guarantees can remain suboptimal in the zero-noise regime, rely on restrictive boundedness conditions (e.g., bounded gradients or objective gaps), use constant or open-loop stepsizes, or require prior knowledge of Lipschitz constants. To overcome these bottlenecks, we introduce OptEMA and analyze two novel variants: OptEMA-M, which applies an adaptive, decreasing EMA coefficient to the first-order moment with a fixed second-order decay, and OptEMA-V, which swaps these roles. Crucially, OptEMA is closed-loop and Lipschitz-free in the sense that its effective stepsizes are trajectory-dependent and do not require the Lipschitz constant for parameterization. Under standard stochastic gradient descent (SGD) assumptions, namely smoothness, a lower-bounded objective, and unbiased gradients with bounded variance, we establish rigorous convergence guarantees. Both variants achieve a noise-adaptive convergence rate of $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/2}+σ^{1/2} T^{-1/4})$ for the average gradient norm, where $σ$ is the noise level. In particular, in the zero-noise regime where $σ=0$, our bounds reduce to the nearly optimal deterministic rate $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(T^{-1/2})$ without manual hyperparameter retuning.

</details>


### [105] [Generative Drifting is Secretly Score Matching: a Spectral and Variational Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09936)
*Erkan Turan,Maks Ovsjanikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为 Drifting 生成模型建立了理论框架，揭示其与得分匹配及梯度流的联系，解决了内核选择和训练稳定性之谜，并提出了加速收敛的优化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 Drifting 生成模型在单步生成中表现出色，但其理论基础尚不明确，尤其是关于内核选择、停止梯度（stop-gradient）操作的必要性以及模型收敛性等关键问题。

Method: 通过线性化 McKean-Vlasov 动力学并在傅里叶空间进行分析，同时将 Drifting 形式化为平滑 KL 散度的 Wasserstein 梯度流（JKO 方案）。

Result: 1) 证明了高斯核下的漂移算子即为得分差；2) 揭示了高斯核的高频瓶颈，解释了拉普拉斯核更优的原因；3) 提出指数级带宽退火策略以加速收敛；4) 证明了 stop-gradient 是 JKO 离散化方案的必然要求。

Conclusion: Drifting 模型本质上是平滑分布下的分分差（score difference），通过 Landau damping 理论和 Wasserstein 梯度流视角，为其内核选择和训练稳定性提供了严密的理论解释。

Abstract: Generative Modeling via Drifting has recently achieved state-of-the-art one-step image generation through a kernel-based drift operator, yet the success is largely empirical and its theoretical foundations remain poorly understood. In this paper, we make the following observation: \emph{under a Gaussian kernel, the drift operator is exactly a score difference on smoothed distributions}. This insight allows us to answer all three key questions left open in the original work: (1) whether a vanishing drift guarantees equality of distributions ($V_{p,q}=0\Rightarrow p=q$), (2) how to choose between kernels, and (3) why the stop-gradient operator is indispensable for stable training. Our observations position drifting within the well-studied score-matching family and enable a rich theoretical perspective. By linearizing the McKean-Vlasov dynamics and probing them in Fourier space, we reveal frequency-dependent convergence timescales comparable to \emph{Landau damping} in plasma kinetic theory: the Gaussian kernel suffers an exponential high-frequency bottleneck, explaining the empirical preference for the Laplacian kernel. We also propose an exponential bandwidth annealing schedule $σ(t)=σ_0 e^{-rt}$ that reduces convergence time from $\exp(O(K_{\max}^2))$ to $O(\log K_{\max})$. Finally, by formalizing drifting as a Wasserstein gradient flow of the smoothed KL divergence, we prove that the stop-gradient operator is derived directly from the frozen-field discretization mandated by the JKO scheme, and removing it severs training from any gradient-flow guarantee. This variational perspective further provides a general template for constructing novel drift operators, demonstrated with a Sinkhorn divergence drift.

</details>


### [106] [SignalMC-MED: A Multimodal Benchmark for Evaluating Biosignal Foundation Models on Single-Lead ECG and PPG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09940)
*Fredrik K. Gustafsson,Xiao Gu,Mattia Carletti,Patitapaban Palo,David W. Eyre,David A. Clifton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文推出 SignalMC-MED 基准，证明了同步 ECG/PPG 多模态融合及长时程数据能显著提升生物信号大模型在 20 项临床任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生物信号大模型缺乏对长时程、多模态同步数据的系统性评估。

Method: 构建了包含 2.2 万余次门诊数据的 SignalMC-MED 基准，涵盖同步 10 分钟 ECG 和 PPG 信号及 20 项临床任务，并系统对比了领域特定模型与通用序列模型。

Result: 领域特定模型优于通用模型；双模态融合优于单模态；10 分钟长信号优于短片段；模型规模增加并不总能带来性能提升；手工特征与模型表征具有互补性。

Conclusion: SignalMC-MED 为评估生物信号大模型提供了标准化基准，并揭示了多模态融合与长时程信号在提升预测精度方面的关键作用。

Abstract: Recent biosignal foundation models (FMs) have demonstrated promising performance across diverse clinical prediction tasks, yet systematic evaluation on long-duration multimodal data remains limited. We introduce SignalMC-MED, a benchmark for evaluating biosignal FMs on synchronized single-lead electrocardiogram (ECG) and photoplethysmogram (PPG) data. Derived from the MC-MED dataset, SignalMC-MED comprises 22,256 visits with 10-minute overlapping ECG and PPG signals, and includes 20 clinically relevant tasks spanning prediction of demographics, emergency department disposition, laboratory value regression, and detection of prior ICD-10 diagnoses. Using this benchmark, we perform a systematic evaluation of representative time-series and biosignal FMs across ECG-only, PPG-only, and ECG + PPG settings. We find that domain-specific biosignal FMs consistently outperform general time-series models, and that multimodal ECG + PPG fusion yields robust improvements over unimodal inputs. Moreover, using the full 10-minute signal consistently outperforms shorter segments, and larger model variants do not reliably outperform smaller ones. Hand-crafted ECG domain features provide a strong baseline and offer complementary value when combined with learned FM representations. Together, these results establish SignalMC-MED as a standardized benchmark and provide practical guidance for evaluating and deploying biosignal FMs.

</details>


### [107] [Task Aware Modulation Using Representation Learning for Upsaling of Terrestrial Carbon Fluxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09974)
*Aleksei Rozanov,Arvind Renganathan,Vipin Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了 TAM-RL 框架，通过引入碳平衡物理约束和时空表征学习，显著提升了全球陆地碳通量估算的准确性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于地面观测数据稀疏且存在区域偏差，现有的数据驱动放大产品在未观测领域泛化性差，导致系统性偏倚和预测不确定性高。

Method: 提出 TAM-RL 框架，将时空表征学习与知识引导的编码器-解码器架构相结合，并引入基于碳平衡方程的损失函数进行物理约束。

Result: 在 150 多个通量塔站点的测试中，TAM-RL 相比现有最先进数据集，将均方根误差（RMSE）降低了 8-9.6%，解释方差（R²）显著提升（最高从 19.4% 增至 43.8%）。

Conclusion: 结合物理约束与自适应表征学习可以显著增强全球碳通量估算的稳健性和迁移能力，为更准确的全球碳收支评估提供了新途径。

Abstract: Accurately upscaling terrestrial carbon fluxes is central to estimating the global carbon budget, yet remains challenging due to the sparse and regionally biased distribution of ground measurements. Existing data-driven upscaling products often fail to generalize beyond observed domains, leading to systematic regional biases and high predictive uncertainty. We introduce Task-Aware Modulation with Representation Learning (TAM-RL), a framework that couples spatio-temporal representation learning with knowledge-guided encoder-decoder architecture and loss function derived from the carbon balance equation. Across 150+ flux tower sites representing diverse biomes and climate regimes, TAM-RL improves predictive performance relative to existing state-of-the-art datasets, reducing RMSE by 8-9.6% and increasing explained variance ($R^2$) from 19.4% to 43.8%, depending on the target flux. These results demonstrate that integrating physically grounded constraints with adaptive representation learning can substantially enhance the robustness and transferability of global carbon flux estimates.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Emotional Modulation in Swarm Decision Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09963)
*David Freire-Obregón*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展蜜蜂决策模型，研究了情绪效价和唤醒度如何通过情绪感染影响群体共识的形成速度与最终倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物与人类群体的集体决策通常由简单规则驱动，但情感状态如何作为调制因素通过影响互动速率进而干扰共识形成的动态过程尚不明确。

Method: 将描述蜜蜂筑巢选择的“蜜蜂方程”扩展为基于主体（Agent-based）的模型，引入情绪效价（正负）和唤醒度（高低）作为调制器，通过模拟面部表情产生的“情绪感染”来改变量化招募和交叉抑制参数。

Result: 情绪调节能通过改变招募和抑制率来偏向决策结果并影响收敛速度；高唤醒度在效价匹配时能打破僵局；同时发现共识形成存在“雪球效应”，即越过特定阈值后决策会加速。

Conclusion: 该框架将群体决策理论与情感建模结合，证明情感不对称与结构性临界点共同决定了集体的选择，为研究生物与人工智能系统的群体决策提供了新工具。

Abstract: Collective decision-making in biological and human groups often emerges from simple interaction rules that amplify minor differences into consensus. The bee equation, developed initially to describe nest-site selection in honeybee swarms, captures this dynamic through recruitment and inhibition processes. Here, we extend the bee equation into an agent-based model in which emotional valence (positive-negative) and arousal (low-high) act as modulators of interaction rates, effectively altering the recruitment and cross-inhibition parameters. Agents display simulated facial expressions mapped from their valence-arousal states, allowing the study of emotional contagion in consensus formation.
  Three scenarios are explored: (1) the joint effect of valence and arousal on consensus outcomes and speed, (2) the role of arousal in breaking ties when valence is matched, and (3) the "snowball effect" in which consensus accelerates after surpassing intermediate support thresholds. Results show that emotional modulation can bias decision outcomes and alter convergence times by shifting effective recruitment and inhibition rates. At the same time, intrinsic non-linear amplification can produce decisive wins even in fully symmetric emotional conditions.
  These findings link classical swarm decision theory with affective and social modelling, highlighting how both emotional asymmetries and structural tipping points shape collective outcomes. The proposed framework offers a flexible tool for studying the emotional dimensions of collective choice in both natural and artificial systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Feedback Does Not Increase the Capacity of Approximately Memoryless Surjective POST Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08886)
*Xiaojing Zhang,Jun Chen,Guanghui Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 研究证明，在几乎所有接近无记忆的POST信道中，由于前一时刻输出对当前状态的影响有限，反馈并不能提升信道容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在非严格无记忆的高级信道模型（如POST信道）中，反馈是否能提高信道容量。

Method: 研究了一类状态由前一时刻输出决定的POST信道（Previous Output as State），通过分析状态依赖转移矩阵的接近程度以及射影条件（surjectivity condition）来推导容量。

Result: 证明了当POST信道接近无记忆且输入字母表大小不小于输出字母表大小时，反馈容量与非反馈容量相等。

Conclusion: 对于接近无记忆且满足射影条件的POST信道，反馈不会增加容量，这证明了香农关于DMC信道的经典结论在更广泛的信道模型下依然成立。

Abstract: We study a class of finite-state channels, known as POST channels, in which the previous channel output serves as the current state. A POST channel is deemed approximately memoryless when the state-dependent transition matrices are sufficiently close to one another. For this family of channels, under a surjectivity condition on the associated memoryless reference channel, we show that the feedback capacity coincides with the non-feedback capacity. Consequently, for almost all approximately memoryless POST channels whose input alphabet size is no smaller than the output alphabet size, feedback provides no capacity gain. This result extends Shannon's classical theorem on discrete memoryless channels and demonstrates that the phenomenon holds well beyond the strictly memoryless case.

</details>


### [110] [A Note on the Equivalence Between Zero-knowledge and Quantum CSS Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08941)
*Noga Ron-Zewi,Mor Weiss*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文揭示了密码学中的零知识编码与量子纠错中的CSS码是等价的，并借此构造了性能优异的零知识局部可测编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在连接密码学中的零知识编码与量子计算中的纠错码这两个看似独立的领域，以利用两者的特性相互促进。

Method: 通过理论分析建立了零知识编码（用于隐藏信息）与量子CSS编码（用于纠错）之间的映射关系。

Result: 利用这种等价性，成功构造出了显式的、渐近良好的零知识局部可测编码（locally-testable codes）。

Conclusion: 证明了(线性、完美)零知识编码与量子CSS编码在数学上是等价的，并展示了这种等价性在构造新型编码中的价值。

Abstract: Zero-knowledge codes, introduced by Decatur, Goldreich, and Ron (ePrint 1997), are error-correcting codes in which few codeword symbols reveal no information about the encoded message, and have been extensively used in cryptographic constructions. Quantum CSS codes, introduced by Calderbank and Shor (Phys. Rev. A 1996) and Steane (Royal Society A 1996), are error-correcting codes that allow for quantum error correction, and are also useful for applications in quantum complexity theory. In this short note, we show that (linear, perfect) zero-knowledge codes and quantum CSS codes are equivalent. We demonstrate the potential of this equivalence by using it to obtain explicit asymptotically-good zero-knowledge locally-testable codes.

</details>


### [111] [Differentially Private Secure Multiplication: Beyond Two Multiplicands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08944)
*Haoyang Hu,Viveck R. Cadambe*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了多节点分布式计算中 M 个私有输入乘积的差异隐私（DP）安全计算问题，提出了分层噪声注入框架，并界定了不同节点规模下的最优隐私与准确度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式计算系统中，当存在节点合谋时，无法同时实现完美的差异隐私（DP）和完美的计算准确度。现有研究仅解决了两个乘数的情况，缺乏对多个乘数（M > 2）情形下的基本隐私-准确度权衡的深入探讨。

Method: 提出了一种基于精心设计的编码多项式（encoding polynomials）结合分层噪声注入（layered noise injection）的安全乘法框架。该方法允许系统性地消除低阶噪声项，从而提高估计精度。

Result: 在 (M-1)T+1 <= N <= MT 范围内，确定了最优的隐私-准确度权衡关系；在 N = T+1 的关键边界情况下，推导出了在高隐私状态下渐近紧致的可实现性界限和逆界。

Conclusion: 本研究将DP安全乘法的理论界限从两个乘数扩展到任意M个，并为分布式系统中不同节点配置下的隐私-准确度权衡关系建立了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We study the problem of differentially private (DP) secure multiplication in distributed computing systems, focusing on regimes where perfect privacy and perfect accuracy cannot be simultaneously achieved. Specifically, N nodes collaboratively compute the product of M private inputs while guaranteeing epsilon-DP against any collusion of up to T nodes. Prior work has characterized the fundamental privacy-accuracy trade-off for the multiplication of two multiplicands. In this paper, we extend these results to the more general setting of computing the product of an arbitrary number M of multiplicands. We propose a secure multiplication framework based on carefully designed encoding polynomials combined with layered noise injection. The proposed construction generalizes existing schemes and enables the systematic cancellation of lower-order noise terms, leading to improved estimation accuracy. We explore two regimes: (M-1)T+1 <= N <= MT and N = T+1. For (M-1)T+1 <= N <= MT, we characterize the optimal privacy--accuracy trade-off. When N = T+1, we derive nontrivial achievability and converse bounds that are asymptotically tight in the high-privacy regime.

</details>


### [112] [Mitigation of UE Antenna Calibration Errors via Differential STBC in Cell-Free Massive MIMO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08962)
*Marx M. M. Freitas,Stefano Buzzi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出利用差分空时分组编码（DSTBC）解决CF-mMIMO系统中用户端天线校准偏差问题，实现无需信道状态信息的可靠下行传输。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对去中心化大规模MIMO（CF-mMIMO）系统中，多天线用户设备（UE）因天线阵列校准受损（如相位偏差）而导致通信性能下降的问题。

Method: 在CF-mMIMO下行链路中引入差分空时分组编码（DSTBC）技术。

Result: 仿真结果表明，DSTBC能有效缓解天线相位偏移的影响，在无需UE端显式校准或信道相位信息的情况下，实现接近相干通信的性能。

Conclusion: 在去中心化大规模MIMO系统中，DSTBC是解决终端侧天线校准受限问题的有效方案，可显著提升系统鲁棒性。

Abstract: This letter investigates the use of differential space-time block coding (DSTBC) to address antenna array calibration impairments at multi-antenna user equipment (UE) in the downlink (DL) of cell-free massive MIMO (CF-mMIMO) systems. We show that, by exploiting DSTBC, reliable DL communication can be achieved without explicit UE-side calibration or channel phase knowledge. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed DSTBC-based transmission effectively mitigates the impact of antenna-dependent phase offsets, restoring near-coherent performance in CF-mMIMO networks.

</details>


### [113] [Do Ambient Backscatter Communication Receivers Require Low-Noise Amplifiers?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09123)
*Xinyi Wang,Yuxin Li,Yinghui Ye,Gongpu Wang,Guangyue Lu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究并证实了低噪声放大器（LNA）在环境反向散射通信中通过能量探测提升性能的有效性，并提出了准最优检测门限的估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在传统点对点通信中有效的低噪声放大器（LNA）是否同样能改善环境反向散射通信（AmBC）中受强干扰影响的符号检测性能。

Method: 构建集成LNA参数的系统模型，推导误码率（BER）与偏转系数（DC），并利用标签导频符号估计参数以设定准最优检测阈值。

Result: 理论分析和DC指标证明，在低至中等发射功率下，LNA能显著增强检测性能；同时得到最小化BER的准最优检测阈值。

Conclusion: 在环境反向散射通信中引入低噪声放大器（LNA）能有效提升能量探测方案的性能，且通过所提算法可以实现准最优检测。

Abstract: In ambient backscatter communication (AmBC), strong direct interference from the ambient source poses a major challenge to reliable symbol detection. Although previous studies have shown that employing a low-noise amplifier (LNA) in conventional point-to-point communication improves symbol detection performance at low-to-moderate transmission power, it remains unclear whether this improvement also holds for AmBC. To respond it, in this work, we investigate the symbol detection performance of an AmBC receiver that is equipped with an LNA and adopts the energy detection (ED) to recover tag's information. Particularly, we first propose a new AmBC symbol detection framework that incorporates LNA parameters. On this basis, we derive the bit error rate (BER) of the ED and employ the deflection coefficient (DC) to demonstrate that the detection performance can be enhanced by the LNA at low-to-moderate ambient source transmission power. Then, we derive the near-optimal detection threshold to minimize the BER and propose a method to estimate the required parameters for this threshold by leveraging the tag's pilot symbols.

</details>


### [114] [Artificial Noise Versus Artificial Noise Elimination: Redefining Scaling Laws of Physical Layer Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09129)
*Hong Niu,Tuo Wu,Xia Lei,Wanbin Tang,Mérouane Debbah,H. Vincent Poor,Chau Yuen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了人工噪声消除（ANE）对无线通信保密率的影响。研究发现，若窃听者天线数超过发射机两倍，安全将无法保障，并提出了在 ANE 存在下维持 AN 有效性的设计准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管人工噪声（AN）是物理层安全的关键技术，但新出现的 AN 消除（ANE）技术威胁其有效性。目前尚缺乏 ANE 对保密率影响的量化研究。

Method: 通过建立平均保密率和瞬时保密率的缩放定律（Scaling Laws）进行理论建模，并推导出各天线数量之间的相互约束关系。

Result: 1. 揭示了发射、接收与窃听天线数量间的互补约束；2. 发现当窃听者天线数超过发射者两倍时，安全通信可能无法保障；3. 确定了 AN 在面对 ANE 攻击时依然有效的充分条件。

Conclusion: AN 在面对高级 ANE 攻击时仍有其存在价值，但需根据发射天线与窃听天线的比例精细设计，以规避通信安全风险。

Abstract: Artificial noise (AN) is a key physical-layer security scheme for wireless communications over multiple-input multiple-output wiretap channels. Recently, artificial noise elimination (ANE) has emerged as a strategy to mitigate the impact of AN on eavesdroppers. However, the influence of ANE on the secrecy rate when counteracting AN has not been investigated. In this paper, we address this issue by establishing scaling laws for both average and instantaneous secrecy rates in the presence of AN and ANE. Based on the scaling laws, several derived corollaries provide insights into the mutual constraints between the number of transmit antennas, receive antennas, and antennas at eavesdroppers, revealing the interplay between these factors. A key corollary reveals that when the eavesdropper possesses more than twice as many antennas as the transmitter, secure communication may no longer be guaranteed. Additionally, by comparing scenarios where ANE counteracts AN with those where AN is not employed, this study identifies sufficient conditions under which AN remains effective. Finally, the derived secrecy rates provide guidelines for system design, even in the presence of advanced ANE countermeasures implemented by the eavesdropper.

</details>


### [115] [Tensor Train Decomposition-based Channel Estimation for MIMO-AFDM Systems with Fractional Delay and Doppler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09293)
*Ruizhe Wang,Cunhua Pan,Hong Ren,Haisu Wu,Jiangzhou Wang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于张量分解的高效MIMO-AFDM信道估计算法，解决了分数延迟带来的参数估计挑战，并在保持高性能的同时显著降低了计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对AFDM系统在实际应用中存在的分数延迟（fractional delay）导致的延迟与多普勒估计精度下降问题，以及现有单符号导频估计方案的局限性。

Method: 提出一种时间-仿射频率（T-AF）域导频结构，并利用空间和时间域的旋转不变性，开发了基于范德蒙德结构张量列（TT）分解的信道估计算法。此外，推导了Ziv-Zakai界（ZZB）以分析性能限。

Result: 所提算法在通信性能上优于现有方案，计算速度提升了一个数量级；推导的ZZB能够更准确地描述低信噪比下的阈值效应。

Conclusion: 该方法不仅能克服分数延迟带来的估计误差，还能兼顾系统实时性，是高移动性MIMO通信中一种极具竞争力的信道估计方案。

Abstract: Affine Frequency Division Multiplexing (AFDM) has emerged as a promising chirp-based multicarrier technology for high-speed communication systems. To fully exploit the diversity gain offered by AFDM, accurate channel estimation is essential. However, existing studies have mainly focused on the integer-delay-tap scenario and single-symbol pilot-based estimation. Since delay taps in practice are generally fractional, approximating them as integers not only degrades delay estimation accuracy but also severely affects Doppler frequency estimation. To address this problem, in this paper, we investigate channel estimation for multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO)-AFDM systems. A time-affine frequency (T-AF) domain pilot structure is proposed to exploit time-domain phase variations. By leveraging the rotational invariance property in the spatial and temporal domains, a channel estimation algorithm based on Vandermonde-structured tensor-train (TT) decomposition is developed. The proposed algorithm demonstrates superior computational efficiency compared with state-of-the-art parameter estimation methods. Moreover, diverging from current studies, we derive the global Ziv-Zakai bound (ZZB) as an alternative parameter estimation error lower bound to the Cramér-Rao bound (CRB). Numerical results show that the derived ZZB provides tighter global performance characterization and successfully captures the threshold phenomenon in mean square error (MSE) performance in the low-SNR regime. Furthermore, the proposed algorithm achieves superior communication performance relative to the existing schemes, while offering a computational speedup, reducing the execution time by an order of magnitude compared to the state-of-the-art iterative algorithms.

</details>


### [116] [Scientific Rigor and Human Warmth: Remembering Vladimir Sidorenko (1949-2025)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09437)
*Christian Deppe,Haider Al Kim,Jessica Bariffi,Hannes Bartz,Minglai Cai,Pau Colomer,Gohar Kyureghyan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文总结了在 FFCS 会议上为已故学者 Dr. Vladimir Sidorenko 举行的纪念活动，回顾了其杰出的学术成就与人格魅力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纪念并致敬在编码理论和电信领域做出杰出贡献的学者 Dr. Vladimir Sidorenko（1949-2025）。

Method: 通过 FFCS 会议的纪念专场，汇集同事、合作者及学生的传记致辞、个人回忆和学术回顾。

Result: 回顾了他在编码理论、密码学、电信和量子纠错领域的广泛影响，以及其超过150篇学术论文的产出。

Conclusion: Dr. Vladimir Sidorenko 不仅在学术上贡献卓著，其人格魅力和对跨学科科研的推动力也为后人留下了宝贵的精神财富。

Abstract: During the Foundations of Future Communication Systems (FFCS) conference in Braunschweig, a dedicated memorial session was held in honor of Dr. Vladimir (Volodya) Sidorenko (1949-2025). The session, chaired by Minglai Cai, brought together colleagues, collaborators, and former students to commemorate his scientific achievements and his exceptional human qualities. This report summarizes the biographical tribute, the personal recollections shared by speakers, and the broader impact of Volodya's work in coding theory, cryptography, telecommunications, and quantum error correction. Beyond his more than 150 publications and substantial technical contributions, the session highlighted his intellectual rigor, mentorship, humor, generosity, and lasting influence on the international research community.

</details>


### [117] [Randomized Distributed Function Computation (RDFC): Ultra-Efficient Semantic Communication Applications to Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09577)
*Onur Günlü*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出随机分布式函数计算（RDFC）框架，通过语义通信将隐私保护（如本地差分隐私）与数据压缩结合，并揭示了共享随机性对显著降低通信速率的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式函数计算中，现有的通信方式往往传输冗余信息。本文旨在建立一种语义通信框架，使发送方仅传输必要信息以生成随机化函数，同时在通信过程中自然地融入安全和隐私约束。

Method: 使用强协调度量（Strong Coordination Metrics）来确保隐私，通过下界分析 Wyner 公共信息（WCI）来评估无共享随机性时的通信成本，并提出数值技术评估有共享随机性下的连续变量速率区域。

Result: 实验显示，充足的共享随机性能将通信速率降低至多两个数量级；即使无共享随机性，RDFC 仍远优于无损传输。此外，有限块长度分析表明隐私参数差距随输入长度呈指数级闭合。

Conclusion: RDFC 被证明是隐私敏感型分布式计算系统中一种高能效的语义通信策略。

Abstract: We establish the randomized distributed function computation (RDFC) framework, in which a sender transmits just enough information for a receiver to generate a randomized function of the input data. Describing RDFC as a form of semantic communication, which can be essentially seen as a generalized remote-source-coding problem, we show that security and privacy constraints naturally fit this model, as they generally require a randomization step. Using strong coordination metrics, we ensure (local differential) privacy for every input sequence and prove that such guarantees can be met even when no common randomness is shared between the transmitter and receiver.
  This work provides lower bounds on Wyner's common information (WCI), which is the communication cost when common randomness is absent, and proposes numerical techniques to evaluate the other corner point of the RDFC rate region for continuous-alphabet random variables with unlimited shared randomness. Experiments illustrate that a sufficient amount of common randomness can reduce the semantic communication rate by up to two orders of magnitude compared to the WCI point, while RDFC without any shared randomness still outperforms lossless transmission by a large margin. A finite blocklength analysis further confirms that the privacy parameter gap between the asymptotic and non-asymptotic RDFC methods closes exponentially fast with input length. Our results position RDFC as an energy-efficient semantic communication strategy for privacy-aware distributed computation systems.

</details>


### [118] [Rate-Distortion Bounds for Heterogeneous Random Fields on Finite Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09833)
*Sujata Sinha,Vishwas Rao,Robert Underwood,David Lenz,Sheng Di,Franck Cappello,Lingjia Liu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个新的有限块长率失真框架，专门用于分析科学计算中分块（tile-based）压缩算法在处理大规模异构随机场时的理论极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的率失真理论主要针对无记忆或统计同质模型，无法准确描述科学计算中处理高维、空间相关且统计异构数据的实际压缩器（如SZ, ZFP）的性能受限情况。

Method: 通过将随机场建模为具有区域平稳二阶统计特性的分段同质模型，并利用非渐进可达性与逆界限（converse bounds），推导出了包含空间相关性、几何形状及瓦片尺寸影响的二阶展开式。

Result: 建立了一个针对有限格点上异构随机场的有限块长率失真框架，量化了瓦片化架构下的率（rate）与离散度（dispersion）。

Conclusion: 本框架揭示了数据异构性和瓦片（tile）几何形状对压缩效率的根本性限制，为科学数据压缩器的架构优化提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Since Shannon's foundational work, rate-distortion theory has defined the fundamental limits of lossy compression. Classical results, derived for memoryless and stationary ergodic sources in the asymptotic regime, have shaped both transform and predictive coding architectures, as well as practical standards such as JPEG. Finite-blocklength refinements, initiated by the non-asymptotic achievability and converse bounds of Kostina and Verdu, provide precise characterizations under excess-distortion probability constraints, but primarily for memoryless or statistically homogeneous models. In contrast, error-bounded practical lossy compressors for scientific computing, such as SZ, ZFP, MGARD, and SPERR, are designed for finite, high-dimensional, spatially correlated, and statistically heterogeneous random fields. These compressors partition data into fixed-size tiles that are processed independently, making tile size a central architectural constraint. Structural heterogeneity, finite lattice effects, and tiling constraints are not addressed by existing finite-blocklength analyses. This paper introduces a finite-blocklength rate-distortion framework for heterogeneous random fields on finite lattices, explicitly accounting for the tile-based architectures used in high-performance scientific compressors. The field is modeled as piecewise homogeneous with regionwise stationary second-order statistics, and tiling constraints are incorporated directly into the source model. Under an excess-distortion probability criterion, we establish non-asymptotic achievability, converse bounds and derive a second-order expansion that quantifies the impact of spatial correlation, region geometry, heterogeneity, and tile size on the rate and dispersion.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [MASEval: Extending Multi-Agent Evaluation from Models to Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08835)
*Cornelius Emde,Alexander Rubinstein,Anmol Goel,Ahmed Heakl,Sangdoo Yun,Seong Joon Oh,Martin Gubri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MASEval 是一个用于系统级评估 LLM Agent 系统的框架。研究发现，开发框架的选择对性能的影响与模型相当，强调了系统组件设计的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然 LLM Agent 框架（如 LangGraph, AutoGen 等）层出不穷，但现有基准测试多以模型为中心，忽略了系统架构（如拓扑结构、编排逻辑、错误处理）对最终性能的重大影响。

Method: 开发了 MASEval，一个框架无关（Framework-agnostic）的库，将整个 Agent 系统视为分析单元。通过在 3 个基准测试、3 个模型和 3 个主流框架上进行系统级对比实验来评估性能。

Result: 实验表明，选择不同的开发框架对性能的影响程度不亚于选择模型。系统实现决策（如编排逻辑）是性能表现的关键变量。

Conclusion: 框架的选择对 Agent 系统性能的影响与模型同样重要。MASEval 填补了系统级评估的空白，为原则性的系统设计和实际落地提供了指导。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of LLM-based agentic systems has produced a rich ecosystem of frameworks (smolagents, LangGraph, AutoGen, CAMEL, LlamaIndex, i.a.). Yet existing benchmarks are model-centric: they fix the agentic setup and do not compare other system components. We argue that implementation decisions substantially impact performance, including choices such as topology, orchestration logic, and error handling. MASEval addresses this evaluation gap with a framework-agnostic library that treats the entire system as the unit of analysis. Through a systematic system-level comparison across 3 benchmarks, 3 models, and 3 frameworks, we find that framework choice matters as much as model choice. MASEval allows researchers to explore all components of agentic systems, opening new avenues for principled system design, and practitioners to identify the best implementation for their use case.
  MASEval is available under the MIT licence https://github.com/parameterlab/MASEval.

</details>


### [120] [Interpretable Markov-Based Spatiotemporal Risk Surfaces for Missing-Child Search Planning with Reinforcement Learning and LLM-Based Quality Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08933)
*Joshua Castillo,Ravi Mukkamala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Guardian 是一个端到端的决策支持系统，利用马尔可夫链、强化学习和大模型三层架构，将非结构化案件数据转化为失踪儿童搜救的地理空间预测和搜索计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 失踪儿童调查的前72小时至关重要，但执法机构面临数据碎片化、非结构化以及缺乏动态地理空间预测工具的挑战。

Method: 提出 Guardian 系统，包含三层预测架构：1. 基于地理属性（道路、隐蔽性等）的马尔可夫链模型；2. 强化学习层，将预测分布转化为搜索计划；3. 大语言模型（LLM）层，负责搜索计划的发布前验证。

Result: 通过合成但真实的案例研究验证，该系统在24/48/72小时的时间跨度内提供了量化输出，结果显示该架构能为区域优化和人工审核提供具有可解释性的先验信息。

Conclusion: Guardian 系统通过三层架构实现了可解释的、操作性强的搜索规划，能有效辅助执法部门进行失踪儿童搜救的早期决策。

Abstract: The first 72 hours of a missing-child investigation are critical for successful recovery. However, law enforcement agencies often face fragmented, unstructured data and a lack of dynamic, geospatial predictive tools. Our system, Guardian, provides an end-to-end decision-support system for missing-child investigation and early search planning. It converts heterogeneous, unstructured case documents into a schema-aligned spatiotemporal representation, enriches cases with geocoding and transportation context, and provides probabilistic search products spanning 0-72 hours. In this paper, we present an overview of Guardian as well as a detailed description of a three-layer predictive component of the system. The first layer is a Markov chain, a sparse, interpretable model with transitions incorporating road accessibility costs, seclusion preferences, and corridor bias with separate day/night parameterizations. The Markov chain's output prediction distributions are then transformed into operationally useful search plans by the second layer's reinforcement learning. Finally, the third layer's LLM performs post hoc validation of layer 2 search plans prior to their release. Using a synthetic but realistic case study, we report quantitative outputs across 24/48/72-hour horizons and analyze sensitivity, failure modes, and tradeoffs. Results show that the proposed predictive system with the three-layer architecture produces interpretable priors for zone optimization and human review.

</details>
